   149→// HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints checks if there are other active sessions
   150→// from the SAME worktree (different from currentSessionID) that have created checkpoints
   151→// on the SAME base commit (current HEAD). This is used to detect concurrent sessions
   152→// in different terminals but same directory.

   154→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {

> AGENT

Here's a summary of when the "another active session" warning is shown:

## When the Warning Appears

The warning is shown when **all** of these conditions are met:

1. **Another session exists** in the same repository
2. **Same worktree** - Both sessions are running from the same working directory  
3. **Same base commit** - Both sessions are based on the same HEAD (current git commit)
4. **Other session has checkpoints** - The other session has created at least one checkpoint (CheckpointCount > 0)
5. **First time detection** - This is the first time the current session has detected the conflict (`ConcurrentWarningShown` flag is not set)

## When the Warning is NOT Shown

- **Different base commits** - Sessions on different HEADs are independent (no conflict)
- **Warning already shown** - Subsequent prompts in the same session proceed normally (both sessions create interleaved checkpoints as promised)
- **No checkpoints yet** - Other session exists but hasn't saved any checkpoints yet
- **Different worktrees** - Sessions in different worktrees/directories don't conflict
- **Warning disabled** - User ran `entire enable --disable-multisession-warning`
- **Strategy doesn't support it** - Currently only `manual-commit` strategy implements this check

## Behavior After Warning

- **First occurrence**: Warning displayed with:
  - Other session's initial prompt (if available)
  - Resume command for the other session
  - Option to continue (both sessions will create interleaved checkpoints)
  - Option to suppress warning in future
  
- **Subsequent prompts**: Proceed normally without showing the warning again

- **Conflict resolved**: If user commits (resolving the base commit conflict), the `ConcurrentWarningShown` flag is automatically cleared

The warning lives in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:113` and `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go:79`, using the strategy method `HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints()` defined in `manual_commit_session.go:149`.

    71→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
    72→//
    73→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
    74→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
    75→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
    76→//
    77→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
    78→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
    79→type StateStore struct {
    80→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
    81→	stateDir string
    82→}
    83→
    84→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
    85→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
    86→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
    87→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→	return &StateStore{
    92→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, sessionStateDirName),
    93→	}, nil
    94→}
    95→
    96→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
    97→// This is useful for testing.
    98→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
    99→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   100→}
   101→
   102→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   103→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   104→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   105→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   106→
   107→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   108→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   109→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   113→
   114→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   115→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   116→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   117→	}
   118→	if err != nil {
   119→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	var state State
   123→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   124→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   125→	}
   126→	return &state, nil
   127→}
   128→
   129→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   130→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   131→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   132→
   133→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   134→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   135→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   139→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   148→
   149→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   150→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   151→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   152→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   153→	}
   154→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→	return nil
   158→}
   159→
   160→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   161→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   162→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   163→
   164→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   165→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   166→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   170→
   171→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   172→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   173→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   174→		}
   175→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   176→	}
   177→	return nil
   178→}
   179→
   180→// List returns all session states.
   181→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   182→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   183→
   184→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   185→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   186→		return nil, nil
   187→	}
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	var states []*State
   193→	for _, entry := range entries {
   194→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   195→			continue
   196→		}
   197→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   198→			continue // Skip temp files
   199→		}
   200→
   201→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   202→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   203→		if err != nil {
   204→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   205→		}
   206→		if state == nil {
   207→			continue
   208→		}
   209→
   210→		states = append(states, state)
   211→	}
   212→	return states, nil
   213→}
   214→
   215→// FindByBaseCommit finds all sessions based on the given commit hash.
   216→func (s *StateStore) FindByBaseCommit(ctx context.Context, baseCommit string) ([]*State, error) {
   217→	allStates, err := s.List(ctx)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		return nil, err
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	var matching []*State
   223→	for _, state := range allStates {
   224→		if state.BaseCommit == baseCommit {
   225→			matching = append(matching, state)
   226→		}
   227→	}
   228→	return matching, nil
   229→}
   230→
   231→// FindByWorktree finds all sessions for the given worktree path.
   232→func (s *StateStore) FindByWorktree(ctx context.Context, worktreePath string) ([]*State, error) {
   233→	allStates, err := s.List(ctx)
   234→	if err != nil {
   235→		return nil, err
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	var matching []*State
   239→	for _, state := range allStates {
   240→		if state.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   241→			matching = append(matching, state)
   242→		}
   243→	}
   244→	return matching, nil
   245→}
   246→
   247→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   248→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   249→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   250→}
   251→
   252→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   253→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   254→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   255→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   256→	ctx := context.Background()
   257→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   258→	cmd.Dir = "."
   259→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   265→
   266→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   267→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   268→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   269→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   273→}
   274→
   275→// GetWorktreePath returns the absolute path to the current worktree root.
   276→func GetWorktreePath() (string, error) {
   277→	ctx := context.Background()
   278→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
   279→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   280→	if err != nil {
   281→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   282→	}
   283→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), nil
   284→}
   285→

    40→}
    41→
    42→// clearSessionState clears session state using the StateStore.
    43→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) clearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
    44→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		return err
    47→	}
    48→	if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
    49→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
    50→	}
    51→	return nil
    52→}
    53→
    54→// listAllSessionStates returns all active session states.
    55→// It filters out orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    56→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listAllSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
    57→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    58→	if err != nil {
    59→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get state store: %w", err)
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	sessionStates, err := store.List(context.Background())
    63→	if err != nil {
    64→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	if len(sessionStates) == 0 {
    68→		return nil, nil
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    72→	if err != nil {
    73→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	var states []*SessionState
    77→	for _, sessionState := range sessionStates {
    78→		state := sessionStateToStrategy(sessionState)
    79→
    80→		// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists
    81→		// Only cleanup if the session has created checkpoints (CheckpointCount > 0)
    82→		// AND has no LastCheckpointID (not recently condensed)
    83→		// Sessions with LastCheckpointID are valid - they were condensed and the shadow
    84→		// branch was intentionally deleted. Keep them for LastCheckpointID reuse.
    85→		shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit)
    86→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    87→		if _, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
    88→			// Shadow branch doesn't exist
    89→			// Only cleanup if session has checkpoints AND no LastCheckpointID
    90→			// Sessions with LastCheckpointID should be kept for checkpoint reuse
    91→			if state.CheckpointCount > 0 && state.LastCheckpointID == "" {
    92→				// Clear the orphaned session state (best-effort, don't fail listing)
    93→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: Cleanup is best-effort, shouldn't fail the list operation
    94→				store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    95→				continue
    96→			}
    97→			// Keep sessions with LastCheckpointID or no checkpoints yet
    98→		}
    99→
   100→		states = append(states, state)
   101→	}
   102→	return states, nil
   103→}
   104→
   105→// findSessionsForWorktree finds all sessions for the given worktree path.
   106→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   107→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return nil, err
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	var matching []*SessionState
   113→	for _, state := range allStates {
   114→		if state.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   115→			matching = append(matching, state)
   116→		}
   117→	}
   118→	return matching, nil
   119→}

> AGENT

## How We Detect Other Sessions

Session detection uses a **filesystem-based tracking system** in `.git/entire-sessions/`:

### 1. Session State Storage

Each active session creates a JSON file in `.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json` containing:

```json
{
  "session_id": "2026-01-23-08940314-9e93-46b1-a598-06bada65ed55",
  "base_commit": "1e17868abc...",
  "worktree_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli",
  "checkpoint_count": 3,
  "concurrent_warning_shown": false,
  "agent_type": "Claude Code",
  "started_at": "2026-01-23T12:00:00Z"
}
```

**Key fields for detection:**
- `base_commit` - HEAD when session started
- `worktree_path` - Absolute path to working directory
- `checkpoint_count` - Number of checkpoints saved
- `concurrent_warning_shown` - Flag to show warning only once

### 2. Storage Location

- **Path**: `.git/entire-sessions/` (in the **common git dir**)
- **Shared across worktrees**: Multiple git worktrees share the same `.git/` directory
- **Atomic writes**: Uses temp file + rename to prevent corruption

### 3. Detection Algorithm

When a session starts or saves a checkpoint, `HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints()` runs:

```go
// Pseudocode
1. Get current worktree path and HEAD commit
2. List all files in .git/entire-sessions/
3. For each session state file:
   - Load and parse JSON
   - Check if shadow branch exists (skip orphaned sessions)
   - Compare against criteria:
     * Different session ID (not current session)
     * Same worktree path (same directory)
     * CheckpointCount > 0 (has saved checkpoints)
     * Same base commit (same HEAD)
4. Return first matching session or nil
```

Implementation in `manual_commit_session.go:149-188`:

```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HasOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    currentWorktree := GetWorktreePath()
    currentHead := repo.Head().Hash().String()
    
    allStates := s.listAllSessionStates() // Reads .git/entire-sessions/*.json
    
    for _, state := range allStates {
        if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&
           state.WorktreePath == currentWorktree &&
           state.CheckpointCount > 0 &&
           state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
            return state, nil // Found conflict
        }
    }
    return nil, nil // No conflict
}
```

### 4. Orphaned Session Cleanup

When listing sessions, the code automatically filters out "orphaned" sessions:

- **Orphaned session**: State file exists but shadow branch (`entire/<commit>`) is missing
- **Cleanup criteria**: `CheckpointCount > 0` AND no `LastCheckpointID`
- **Why keep some**: Sessions with `LastCheckpointID` were already condensed - shadow branch deletion is intentional

### 5. Cross-Terminal Detection

This system detects sessions across:
- **Multiple terminal windows** - All read from same `.git/entire-sessions/`
- **Different AI agents** - Claude Code vs Gemini CLI sessions are detected via `agent_type` field
- **Shared worktrees** - Git worktrees share the common `.git/` directory

### 6. Lifecycle

```
Session starts → Create .git/entire-sessions/<id>.json
  ↓
Checkpoint saved → Increment checkpoint_count in state file
  ↓
User commits → Shadow branch condensed to entire/sessions
  ↓
Session ends → State file remains (tracks LastCheckpointID for reuse)
  ↓
User starts new session on different commit → Old state becomes irrelevant (different base_commit)
```

The state files persist after sessions end to enable checkpoint ID reuse and prevent unnecessary condensation on subsequent commits.

> AGENT

## Why This Happens Mid-Session

The key is on **line 183**: `state.CheckpointCount > 0`

The detection **only triggers when the other session has saved at least one checkpoint**. Here's the exact scenario:

### Timeline

1. **Background session opens** (e.g., VS Code still has Claude open, or terminal window minimized)
   - Creates state file: `.git/entire-sessions/session-A.json`
   - `CheckpointCount: 0` (no checkpoints saved yet)

2. **Active session starts & runs several prompts**
   - Creates state file: `.git/entire-sessions/session-B.json`
   - Saves checkpoints normally (increments its own CheckpointCount)
   - **No warning shown** because Session A has `CheckpointCount: 0`

3. **Background session finally saves a checkpoint**
   - Session A updates its state: `CheckpointCount: 1`
   - This happens when background Claude responds to a previous prompt or auto-saves

4. **Active session's next prompt triggers detection**
   - Reads Session A's state file
   - Sees `CheckpointCount: 1` → **Warning triggered!**

### Why the Delay?

The background session might have been:
- **Idle during prompt input** - State file exists but no checkpoints yet
- **Processing a slow response** - Checkpoint saved after your active session started
- **Open but inactive** - Finally saved a checkpoint when you weren't looking

### The Race Condition

```
Time  | Background Session        | Active Session
------|---------------------------|---------------------------
T0    | Opens (CheckpointCount=0) |
T1    |                           | Opens (CheckpointCount=0)
T2    |                           | Prompt 1 → checkpoint saved
T3    |                           | Check: BG has 0 → ✅ no warning
T4    |                           | Prompt 2 → checkpoint saved
T5    |                           | Check: BG has 0 → ✅ no warning
T6    | Saves checkpoint (=1)     |
T7    |                           | Prompt 3 → checkpoint saved
T8    |                           | Check: BG has 1 → ⚠️ WARNING!
```

This explains why the user got the warning "after running a few prompts" - the background session was open but hadn't saved any checkpoints until midway through the active session.

## Session ID Format

Defined in `paths/paths.go:217-220`:

```go
// EntireSessionID generates the full Entire session ID from a Claude session ID.
// The format is: YYYY-MM-DD-<claude-session-id>
func EntireSessionID(claudeSessionID string) string {
	return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02") + "-" + claudeSessionID
}
```

## Structure

```
YYYY-MM-DD-<agent-session-uuid>
│          │
│          └─ Agent's session ID (UUID from Claude/Gemini)
└─ Date when session was created
```

**Example:** `2026-01-23-08940314-9e93-46b1-a598-06bada65ed55`
- Date: `2026-01-23`
- Agent UUID: `08940314-9e93-46b1-a598-06bada65ed55`

## Benefits

1. **Human-readable** - Can see when a session was created at a glance
2. **Sortable** - Lexicographic sort = chronological sort
3. **Debuggable** - Easy to identify recent vs old sessions in `.git/entire-sessions/`
4. **Extractable** - Can strip the date prefix to get the agent's original session ID using `ModelSessionID()` (line 225)

> AGENT

Found it! The bug is on **line 181** in `manual_commit_session.go`:

```go
if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&
```

## The Problem

When a user resumes the same Claude session across midnight:

**Day 1 (Jan 22):**
- Claude session UUID: `a6c3cac2-2f45-43aa-8c69-419f66a3b5e1`
- Entire session ID: `2026-01-22-a6c3cac2-2f45-43aa-8c69-419f66a3b5e1`
- Creates state file with CheckpointCount > 0

**Day 2 (Jan 23) - Same Claude session resumes:**
- Claude session UUID: `a6c3cac2-2f45-43aa-8c69-419f66a3b5e1` (unchanged)
- Entire session ID: `2026-01-23-a6c3cac2-2f45-43aa-8c69-419f66a3b5e1` (date changed!)
- Detection sees different session IDs → triggers warning

## The Fix

Compare the **agent session UUIDs** (without date prefix) instead of full Entire session IDs:

```go
// Extract agent UUIDs (strip date prefix)
currentAgentID := paths.ModelSessionID(currentSessionID)
otherAgentID := paths.ModelSessionID(state.SessionID)

for _, state := range allStates {
    if otherAgentID != currentAgentID &&  // Compare UUIDs, not full IDs
       state.WorktreePath == currentWorktree &&
       state.CheckpointCount > 0 &&
       state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
        return state, nil
    }
}
```

This way, the same Claude session resumed across midnight will be recognized as the same session and won't trigger a false warning.

> AGENT

Found **3 critical bugs** where we compare full Entire session IDs (with date prefix) when we should compare agent UUIDs:

## 1. **Concurrent Session Detection** (Already Identified)

**File:** `strategy/manual_commit_session.go:181`

```go
if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&  // BUG: compares full IDs
```

**Impact:** False warning when same Claude session resumes across midnight.

---

## 2. **Rewind List Filtering** 

**File:** `checkpoint/temporary.go:428`

```go
if sessionID != "" && commitSessionID != sessionID {
    return nil // Skip commits from other sessions
}
```

Called from `manual_commit_rewind.go:59`:
```go
checkpoints, err := store.ListTemporaryCheckpoints(ctx, state.BaseCommit, state.SessionID, limit)
```

**Impact:** Old checkpoints from before midnight are **hidden from rewind list**. User can't see or rewind to checkpoints created in the same session earlier that day.

---

## 3. **Shadow Branch Orphan Detection**

**File:** `strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go:761`

```go
if found && existingSessionID != sessionID {  // BUG: compares full IDs
    existingState, _ := s.loadSessionState(existingSessionID)
    if existingState == nil {
        // Orphaned - reset branch
    } else {
        // CONFLICT - blocks session
        return &SessionIDConflictError{...}
    }
}
```

**Impact:** When same Claude session resumes across midnight:
- Old shadow branch has: `Entire-Session: 2026-01-22-a6c3cac2...`
- New session: `2026-01-23-a6c3cac2...`
- Old state file **still exists**: `.git/entire-sessions/2026-01-22-a6c3cac2....json`
- Code thinks it's a conflict → **blocks the session entirely** with `SessionIDConflictError`

---

## 4. **Checkpoint Lookup** (Might be OK)

**File:** `strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go:62`

```go
if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
```

This has a **prefix match** which might be intentional to support partial session IDs, but could still cause issues with exact matching scenarios.

---

## Root Cause

All bugs stem from using the **full Entire session ID** (format: `YYYY-MM-DD-<uuid>`) for identity comparisons, when the actual session identity is the **agent UUID** (which stays constant across days).

**State file storage makes it worse:** Each day creates a **different state file**:
- `.git/entire-sessions/2026-01-22-a6c3cac2....json` 
- `.git/entire-sessions/2026-01-23-a6c3cac2....json`

This causes the orphan detection to think the old state is from a "different" session.

> DEVELOPER

can we fix it that it keeps using the old state file?

> AGENT

Yes! That's actually a **cleaner fix** than changing all the comparison logic. Instead of stripping dates everywhere, we make the session ID **stable** by reusing the existing state file.

Let me check where session IDs are generated:

> AGENT

Perfect approach! Instead of fixing all the comparison logic, we make the session ID **stable** by reusing existing state files.

## The Fix

Create a new function that checks for existing sessions before generating a new ID:

```go
// In strategy/session_state.go or paths/paths.go

// GetOrCreateEntireSessionID returns a stable session ID for the given agent UUID.
// If a session state already exists with this UUID, returns that session ID
// (preserving the original date prefix). Otherwise creates a new ID with today's date.
func GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) (string, error) {
    stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    if err != nil {
        return "", err
    }
    
    // Check if state directory exists
    entries, err := os.ReadDir(stateDir)
    if err != nil {
        if os.IsNotExist(err) {
            // First session ever - create new ID
            return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID), nil
        }
        return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
    }
    
    // Look for existing state file with matching agent UUID
    for _, entry := range entries {
        if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
            continue
        }
        
        existingSessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
        existingUUID := paths.ModelSessionID(existingSessionID)
        
        if existingUUID == agentSessionUUID {
            // Found existing session - reuse its ID (with original date)
            return existingSessionID, nil
        }
    }
    
    // No existing session found - create new ID with today's date
    return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID), nil
}
```

Then update the hook handlers:

```go
// In hooks_claudecode_handlers.go and hooks_geminicli_handlers.go

// OLD:
entireSessionID := paths.EntireSessionID(input.SessionID)

// NEW:
entireSessionID, err := strategy.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(input.SessionID)
if err != nil {
    return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session ID: %w", err)
}
```

## Benefits

1. **Session ID stays constant** - `2026-01-22-a6c3cac2...` even on Jan 23+
2. **One state file per session** - No proliferation of files
3. **No comparison logic changes needed** - All existing code works
4. **Semantically correct** - Date represents "session start date"
5. **Fixes all 3 bugs** automatically:
   - Concurrent session detection ✅
   - Rewind list filtering ✅
   - Shadow branch orphan detection ✅

## Edge Cases

- **State file deleted manually**: Next day creates NEW session ID - acceptable recovery
- **Session ends and restarts**: New session ID (correct behavior)
- **Cross-worktree conflict**: Still detected correctly (different worktree paths)

This is much cleaner than stripping dates everywhere!

> AGENT

Yes, there's an **easy fix**! Since the date format is `YYYY-MM-DD`, lexicographic sorting gives us the most recent date automatically:

```go
// GetOrCreateEntireSessionID returns a stable session ID for the given agent UUID.
// If multiple state files exist (due to bug), picks the most recent by date.
func GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) (string, error) {
    stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    if err != nil {
        return "", err
    }
    
    entries, err := os.ReadDir(stateDir)
    if err != nil {
        if os.IsNotExist(err) {
            return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID), nil
        }
        return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
    }
    
    // Collect all matching session IDs
    var matches []string
    for _, entry := range entries {
        if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
            continue
        }
        
        existingSessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
        existingUUID := paths.ModelSessionID(existingSessionID)
        
        if existingUUID == agentSessionUUID {
            matches = append(matches, existingSessionID)
        }
    }
    
    if len(matches) == 0 {
        return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID), nil
    }
    
    // Pick most recent (YYYY-MM-DD sorts correctly)
    sort.Strings(matches)
    return matches[len(matches)-1], nil
}
```

**Why it works:**
- `2026-01-22-uuid` < `2026-01-23-uuid` (lexicographic sort)
- Last element = most recent date
- Only ~10 lines of extra code

**Optional cleanup** (if you want to be nice):
```go
// After picking the most recent:
mostRecent := matches[len(matches)-1]

// Best-effort cleanup of old duplicates
for _, oldID := range matches[:len(matches)-1] {
    os.Remove(filepath.Join(stateDir, oldID+".json")) // Ignore errors
}

return mostRecent, nil
```

The cleanup is optional since old state files are harmless (they'll just sit there unused). But it keeps the directory tidy.

**Do you want the cleanup included?** It's 3 extra lines but prevents accumulation of dead state files.

    56→	var state SessionState
    57→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→	return &state, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// SaveSessionState saves the session state atomically.
    64→func SaveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    65→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
    66→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
    67→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state directory: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	if err := os.MkdirAll(stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
    76→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(state.SessionID)
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
    90→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
    91→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
    92→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
    93→	}
    94→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
    95→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→	return nil
    98→}
    99→
   100→// ClearSessionState removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   101→func ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   102→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   103→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   104→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(sessionID)
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   113→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   114→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   115→		}
   116→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→	return nil
   119→}
   120→

  "new_string": "// sessionStateFile returns the path to a session state file.\nfunc sessionStateFile(sessionID string) (string, error) {\n\tstateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+\".json\"), nil\n}\n\n// GetOrCreateEntireSessionID returns a stable session ID for the given agent UUID.\n// If a session state already exists with this UUID, returns that session ID\n// (preserving the original date prefix). Otherwise creates a new ID with today's date.\n//\n// When multiple state files exist for the same UUID (due to the midnight-crossing bug),\n// this function picks the most recent by date and cleans up older duplicates.\nfunc GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) (string, error) {\n\tstateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\n\tentries, err := os.ReadDir(stateDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\t// First session ever - create new ID\n\t\t\treturn paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID), nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read session state directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Collect all matching session IDs\n\tvar matches []string\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), \".json\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\texistingSessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), \".json\")\n\t\texistingUUID := paths.ModelSessionID(existingSessionID)\n\n\t\tif existingUUID == agentSessionUUID {\n\t\t\tmatches = append(matches, existingSessionID)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif len(matches) == 0 {\n\t\t// No existing session found - create new ID with today's date\n\t\treturn paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID), nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Pick most recent (YYYY-MM-DD sorts correctly lexicographically)\n\tsort.Strings(matches)\n\tmostRecent := matches[len(matches)-1]\n\n\t// Best-effort cleanup of old duplicates (ignore errors)\n\tfor _, oldID := range matches[:len(matches)-1] {\n\t\toldFile := filepath.Join(stateDir, oldID+\".json\")\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(oldFile) //nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: best-effort cleanup\n\t}\n\n\treturn mostRecent, nil\n}"
}

    37→// GetOrCreateEntireSessionID returns a stable session ID for the given agent UUID.
    38→// If a session state already exists with this UUID, returns that session ID
    39→// (preserving the original date prefix). Otherwise creates a new ID with today's date.
    40→//
    41→// When multiple state files exist for the same UUID (due to the midnight-crossing bug),
    42→// this function picks the most recent by date and cleans up older duplicates.
    43→func GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) (string, error) {
    44→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		return "", err
    47→	}

    58→	// Collect all matching session IDs

    60→	for _, entry := range entries {
    61→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→
    65→		existingSessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")

    69→			matches = append(matches, existingSessionID)
    70→		}
    71→	}

    74→		// No existing session found - create new ID with today's date
    75→		return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID), nil
    76→	}

    79→	sort.Strings(matches)

    85→		_ = os.Remove(oldFile) //nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: best-effort cleanup

    89→}

err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SaveSessionState() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Second call - should reuse existing session ID\n\tsessionID2, err := GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentUUID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetOrCreateEntireSessionID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif sessionID2 != sessionID1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected to reuse session ID %s, got %s\", sessionID1, sessionID2)\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates tests cleanup of duplicate state files.\nfunc TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\t_, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to init git repo: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\tagentUUID := \"b7d4dbd3-3e56-54bb-a70a-52ae77d94c6f\"\n\n\t// Simulate the bug: create state files from different days\n\toldSessionID := \"2026-01-22-\" + agentUUID\n\tnewSessionID := \"2026-01-23-\" + agentUUID\n\n\toldState := &SessionState{\n\t\tSessionID:       oldSessionID,\n\t\tBaseCommit:      \"old123\",\n\t\tStartedAt:       time.Now().Add(-24 * time.Hour),\n\t\tCheckpointCount: 2,\n\t}\n\tif err := SaveSessionState(oldState); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SaveSessionState(old) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnewState := &SessionState{\n\t\tSessionID:       newSessionID,\n\t\tBaseCommit:      \"new456\",\n\t\tStartedAt:       time.Now(),\n\t\tCheckpointCount: 3,\n\t}\n\tif err := SaveSessionState(newState); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"SaveSessionState(new) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Call GetOrCreateEntireSessionID - should pick the newest and cleanup old\n\tselectedID, err := GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentUUID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GetOrCreateEntireSessionID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Should pick the most recent (2026-01-23)\n\tif selectedID != newSessionID {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected most recent session ID %s, got %s\", newSessionID, selectedID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Old state file should be cleaned up\n\toldStateLoaded, err := LoadSessionState(oldSessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"LoadSessionState(old) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif oldStateLoaded != nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Old state file should have been cleaned up, but still exists\")\n\t}\n\n\t// New state file should still exist\n\tnewStateLoaded, err := LoadSessionState(newSessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"LoadSessionState(new) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif newStateLoaded == nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"New state file should exist\")\n\t}\n}"

> AGENT

Perfect! Let me create a summary of the changes:

## Summary

I've fixed the midnight-crossing session ID bug by making session IDs **stable** across days. Here's what was implemented:

### The Fix

**New Function:** `strategy.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string)`
- Checks for existing state files with the same agent UUID
- If found, **reuses the existing session ID** (preserving original date)
- If multiple exist (due to the bug), picks the most recent and cleans up old ones
- If none exist, creates new ID with today's date

### Files Changed

1. **`strategy/session_state.go`**
   - Added `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` function with cleanup logic
   - Added imports for `sort` and `strings`

2. **`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`** (2 locations updated)
   - `handleClaudeSessionStart()`: Use new function instead of `paths.EntireSessionID()`
   - `currentSessionIDWithFallback()`: Use new function in fallback path

3. **`hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`** (2 locations updated)
   - `handleGeminiSessionStart()`: Use new function
   - `handleGeminiBeforeAgent()`: Use new function

4. **`strategy/session_state_test.go`**
   - Added `TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID()`: Tests basic functionality
   - Added `TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates()`: Tests duplicate cleanup

### What This Fixes

All 3 bugs identified earlier are now resolved:

1. ✅ **Concurrent session warning** - Same session won't trigger false warning
2. ✅ **Rewind list filtering** - All checkpoints from same session are visible
3. ✅ **Shadow branch orphan detection** - Session recognized as same, no false conflict

### Behavior

**Before:**
- Jan 22: Session ID = `2026-01-22-a6c3cac2...`, state file created
- Jan 23: Same Claude session → NEW session ID = `2026-01-23-a6c3cac2...`, NEW state file
- Result: System thinks these are different sessions ❌

**After:**
- Jan 22: Session ID = `2026-01-22-a6c3cac2...`, state file created
- Jan 23: Same Claude session → **REUSES** `2026-01-22-a6c3cac2...`
- Result: System correctly recognizes same session ✅

**Cleanup:** If both state files exist (bug already occurred), it automatically picks the newest and deletes the old one.

All tests pass! ✅

> AGENT

Perfect! You were absolutely right. I've updated the code so that `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` now:

1. **Never returns an error** - it always falls back to generating a new ID if anything goes wrong
2. **Handles all error cases internally** - whether it's a missing state dir, permission issues, or corrupt git repo

This makes all the callers much cleaner:

**Before:**
```go
entireSessionID, err := strategy.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(input.SessionID)
if err != nil {
    // Fall back to generating new ID
    fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get stable session ID: %v\n", err)
    return paths.EntireSessionID(modelSessionID)
}
```

**After:**
```go
entireSessionID := strategy.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(input.SessionID)
```

Much cleaner! All tests still pass ✅

> DEVELOPER

would it not make sense to keep this on the paths package?

> AGENT

Good question! You're right that it conceptually feels like it belongs with `paths.EntireSessionID()` and `paths.ModelSessionID()`. However, there's a **circular dependency problem**:

**Current imports:**
- `strategy` package imports `paths` package
- `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` needs:
  - `getSessionStateDir()` (from `strategy` package) 
  - Access to `.git/entire-sessions/` state files

**If we move it to `paths`:**
- `paths` would need to import `strategy` (to access session state directory logic)
- This creates: `paths` → `strategy` → `paths` (circular dependency ❌)

**Options:**

1. **Keep it in `strategy`** (current) - Works but less discoverable
2. **Move to `paths` with inline logic** - Duplicate `getSessionStateDir()` logic in paths package (code duplication)
3. **Create new `session` package** - Move session-related logic there (bigger refactor)
4. **Move `getSessionStateDir()` to `paths`** - Break circular dependency (cleaner, but affects other strategy code)

**My recommendation:** Keep it in `strategy` for now since:
- It's tightly coupled to session state file management
- The `.git/entire-sessions/` directory is a strategy implementation detail
- Callers already import `strategy` for other session operations

But if you want it more discoverable, we could add a **wrapper in `paths`**:

```go
// In paths/paths.go
func GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) string {
    return strategy.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID)
}
```

This gives you the discoverable API in `paths` without circular dependencies. What do you think?

> AGENT

It's actually quite simple! `getSessionStateDir()` just:

1. Calls `GetGitCommonDir()` which runs: `git rev-parse --git-common-dir`
2. Appends `/entire-sessions`
3. Returns `.git/entire-sessions/`

So it's basically just **path construction** to find `.git/entire-sessions/`.

You're right - we **could** move `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` to `paths` because:
- The git common dir lookup is simple (just one git command)
- The constant `"entire-sessions"` could live in `paths` (or be passed as an arg)
- No real strategy-specific logic

**Would you like me to move it to `paths`?** I'd need to either:
1. **Move `GetGitCommonDir()` to `paths` too** (cleaner)
2. **Inline the git command in `paths`** (duplicates the command)
3. **Pass state dir as parameter** (caller has to know implementation detail)

Option 1 seems cleanest - `GetGitCommonDir()` is general-purpose enough to live in `paths`. What do you think?

package jsonutil

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
)

// MarshalIndentWithNewline is like json.MarshalIndent but adds a trailing newline.
// This ensures JSON files have proper POSIX line endings.
func MarshalIndentWithNewline(v any, prefix, indent string) ([]byte, error) {
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	enc := json.NewEncoder(&buf)
	enc.SetIndent(prefix, indent)

> AGENT

Here's a quick summary of the package scopes:

## Core Domain Packages

**`session/`** - Session domain logic
- High-level session CRUD operations
- StateStore for managing `.git/entire-sessions/` state files
- Combines session state + checkpoint data

**`checkpoint/`** - Checkpoint storage abstraction
- Temporary checkpoints (shadow branches)
- Committed checkpoints (metadata branches)
- Storage operations (read/write/list)

**`strategy/`** - Git strategy implementations
- Manual-commit, auto-commit strategies
- Implements save/rewind/condense logic
- Session state management (currently lives here)

**`agent/`** - Agent integration layer
- Abstracts Claude Code, Gemini CLI, etc.
- Hook parsing, session ID transformation
- Agent detection and configuration

## Utility Packages

**`paths/`** - Path construction & constants
- `.entire/`, `.git/` directory paths
- Session ID formatting (`EntireSessionID`, `ModelSessionID`)
- File path helpers

**`trailers/`** - Git commit trailer parsing
**`logging/`** - Structured logging (slog)
**`jsonutil/`, `stringutil/`, `textutil/`** - Simple utilities

---

## Where should `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` live?

**Option 1: `session/` package** ✅ **BEST FIT**
- It manages session identity/lifecycle
- `StateStore` already manages `.git/entire-sessions/`
- Natural home for "find or create session ID" logic

**Option 2: `strategy/` package** (current)
- Works but conceptually wrong - it's not strategy-specific
- Strategies use sessions, not define them

**Option 3: `paths/` package**
- Wrong - `paths` is for path construction, not session lifecycle

**Recommendation:** Move to `session/` package. It's the right domain owner for session identity.

    24→// State represents the state of an active session.
    25→// This is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
    26→type State struct {
    27→	// SessionID is the unique session identifier
    28→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    29→
    30→	// BaseCommit is the HEAD commit when the session started
    31→	BaseCommit string `json:"base_commit"`
    32→
    33→	// WorktreePath is the absolute path to the worktree root
    34→	WorktreePath string `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"`
    35→
    36→	// StartedAt is when the session was started
    37→	StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at"`
    38→
    39→	// CheckpointCount is the number of checkpoints created in this session
    40→	CheckpointCount int `json:"checkpoint_count"`
    41→
    42→	// CondensedTranscriptLines tracks lines already included in previous condensation
    43→	CondensedTranscriptLines int `json:"condensed_transcript_lines,omitempty"`
    44→
    45→	// UntrackedFilesAtStart tracks files that existed at session start (to preserve during rewind)
    46→	UntrackedFilesAtStart []string `json:"untracked_files_at_start,omitempty"`
    47→
    48→	// FilesTouched tracks files modified/created/deleted during this session
    49→	FilesTouched []string `json:"files_touched,omitempty"`
    50→
    51→	// ConcurrentWarningShown is true if user was warned about concurrent sessions
    52→	ConcurrentWarningShown bool `json:"concurrent_warning_shown,omitempty"`
    53→
    54→	// LastCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID from last condensation, reused for subsequent commits without new content
    55→	LastCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:"last_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    56→
    57→	// AgentType identifies the agent that created this session (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI", "Cursor")
    58→	AgentType agent.AgentType `json:"agent_type,omitempty"`
    59→
    60→	// Token usage tracking (accumulated across all checkpoints in this session)
    61→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
    62→
    63→	// Transcript position when session started (for multi-session checkpoints)
    64→	TranscriptLinesAtStart int    `json:"transcript_lines_at_start,omitempty"`
    65→	TranscriptUUIDAtStart  string `json:"transcript_uuid_at_start,omitempty"`
    66→
    67→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection)
    68→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path,omitempty"`
    69→}

    76→//
    77→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
    78→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.

    84→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
    85→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
    86→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
    87→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→	return &StateStore{
    92→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, sessionStateDirName),
    93→	}, nil
    94→}
    95→
    96→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
    97→// This is useful for testing.
    98→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
    99→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   100→}
   101→
   102→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   103→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   104→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   105→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   106→
   107→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   108→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   109→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   113→
   114→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   115→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   116→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   117→	}
   118→	if err != nil {
   119→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	var state State
   123→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   124→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   125→	}
   126→	return &state, nil
   127→}

   255→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   256→	ctx := context.Background()
   257→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   258→	cmd.Dir = "."
   259→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   260→	if err != nil {
   261→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   265→
   266→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   267→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   268→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   269→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   273→}

  "new_string": "// GetWorktreePath returns the absolute path to the current worktree root.\nfunc GetWorktreePath() (string, error) {\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"--show-toplevel\")\n\toutput, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get worktree path: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), nil\n}\n\n// GetOrCreateEntireSessionID returns a stable session ID for the given agent UUID.\n// If a session state already exists with this UUID, returns that session ID\n// (preserving the original date prefix). Otherwise creates a new ID with today's date.\n//\n// When multiple state files exist for the same UUID (due to the midnight-crossing bug),\n// this function picks the most recent by date and cleans up older duplicates.\n//\n// This function never returns an error - it always falls back to generating a new ID\n// if any issues occur (e.g., corrupt git repo, permission problems).\nfunc GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) string {\n\tcommonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Can't get common dir (corrupt git repo?) - fall back to new ID\n\t\treturn paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID)\n\t}\n\n\tstateDir := filepath.Join(commonDir, sessionStateDirName)\n\tentries, err := os.ReadDir(stateDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// State dir doesn't exist or can't read it - fall back to new ID\n\t\treturn paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Collect all matching session IDs\n\tvar matches []string\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), \".json\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\texistingSessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), \".json\")\n\t\texistingUUID := paths.ModelSessionID(existingSessionID)\n\n\t\tif existingUUID == agentSessionUUID {\n\t\t\tmatches = append(matches, existingSessionID)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif len(matches) == 0 {\n\t\t// No existing session found - create new ID with today's date\n\t\treturn paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Pick most recent (YYYY-MM-DD sorts correctly lexicographically)\n\tsort.Strings(matches)\n\tmostRecent := matches[len(matches)-1]\n\n\t// Best-effort cleanup of old duplicates (ignore errors)\n\tfor _, oldID := range matches[:len(matches)-1] {\n\t\toldFile := filepath.Join(stateDir, oldID+\".json\")\n\t\t_ = os.Remove(oldFile)\n\t}\n\n\treturn mostRecent\n}"
}

    10→
    11→	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    12→	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// Session state management functions shared across all strategies.
    16→// SessionState is stored in .git/entire-sessions/{session_id}.json
    17→
    18→// getSessionStateDir returns the path to the session state directory.
    19→// This is stored in the git common dir so it's shared across all worktrees.
    20→func getSessionStateDir() (string, error) {
    21→	commonDir, err := GetGitCommonDir()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return "", err
    24→	}
    25→	return filepath.Join(commonDir, sessionStateDirName), nil
    26→}
    27→
    28→// sessionStateFile returns the path to a session state file.
    29→func sessionStateFile(sessionID string) (string, error) {
    30→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    31→	if err != nil {
    32→		return "", err
    33→	}
    34→	return filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+".json"), nil
    35→}
    36→
    37→// GetOrCreateEntireSessionID returns a stable session ID for the given agent UUID.
    38→// If a session state already exists with this UUID, returns that session ID
    39→// (preserving the original date prefix). Otherwise creates a new ID with today's date.
    40→//
    41→// When multiple state files exist for the same UUID (due to the midnight-crossing bug),
    42→// this function picks the most recent by date and cleans up older duplicates.
    43→//
    44→// This function never returns an error - it always falls back to generating a new ID
    45→// if any issues occur (e.g., corrupt git repo, permission problems).
    46→func GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) string {
    47→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		// Can't get state dir (corrupt git repo?) - fall back to new ID
    50→		return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID)
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(stateDir)
    54→	if err != nil {
    55→		// State dir doesn't exist or can't read it - fall back to new ID
    56→		return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID)
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Collect all matching session IDs
    60→	var matches []string
    61→	for _, entry := range entries {
    62→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
    63→			continue
    64→		}
    65→
    66→		existingSessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
    67→		existingUUID := paths.ModelSessionID(existingSessionID)
    68→
    69→		if existingUUID == agentSessionUUID {
    70→			matches = append(matches, existingSessionID)
    71→		}
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	if len(matches) == 0 {
    75→		// No existing session found - create new ID with today's date
    76→		return paths.EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID)
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Pick most recent (YYYY-MM-DD sorts correctly lexicographically)
    80→	sort.Strings(matches)
    81→	mostRecent := matches[len(matches)-1]
    82→
    83→	// Best-effort cleanup of old duplicates (ignore errors)
    84→	for _, oldID := range matches[:len(matches)-1] {
    85→		oldFile := filepath.Join(stateDir, oldID+".json")
    86→		_ = os.Remove(oldFile)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	return mostRecent
    90→}
    91→
    92→// LoadSessionState loads the session state for the given session ID.
    93→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
    94→func LoadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    95→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
    96→	if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
    97→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
    98→	}

err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Save() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Second call - should reuse existing session ID\n\tsessionID2 := GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentUUID)\n\n\tif sessionID2 != sessionID1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected to reuse session ID %s, got %s\", sessionID1, sessionID2)\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates tests cleanup of duplicate state files.\nfunc TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\t_, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to init git repo: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.Chdir(dir); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to change directory: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tagentUUID := \"b7d4dbd3-3e56-54bb-a70a-52ae77d94c6f\"\n\n\t// Simulate the bug: create state files from different days\n\toldSessionID := \"2026-01-22-\" + agentUUID\n\tnewSessionID := \"2026-01-23-\" + agentUUID\n\n\tstore, err := NewStateStore()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"NewStateStore() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\toldState := &State{\n\t\tSessionID:       oldSessionID,\n\t\tBaseCommit:      \"old123\",\n\t\tStartedAt:       time.Now().Add(-24 * time.Hour),\n\t\tCheckpointCount: 2,\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Save(nil, oldState); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Save(new) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Call GetOrCreateEntireSessionID - should pick the newest and cleanup old\n\tselectedID := GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentUUID)\n\n\t// Should pick the most recent (2026-01-23)\n\tif selectedID != newSessionID {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Expected most recent session ID %s, got %s\", newSessionID, selectedID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Old state file should be cleaned up\n\toldStateLoaded, err := store.Load(nil, oldSessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Load(old) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif oldStateLoaded != nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Old state file should have been cleaned up, but still exists\")\n\t}\n\n\t// New state file should still exist\n\tnewStateLoaded, err := store.Load(nil, newSessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Load(new) error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif newStateLoaded == nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"New state file should exist\")\n\t}\n}"

   149→// TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID tests the stable session ID generation logic.
   150→func TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID(t *testing.T) {
   151→	dir := t.TempDir()
   152→	_, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	t.Chdir(dir)
   158→
   159→	agentUUID := "a6c3cac2-2f45-43aa-8c69-419f66a3b5e1"
   160→
   161→	// First call - should create new session ID with today's date
   162→	sessionID1 := GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentUUID)
   163→
   164→	// Verify format: YYYY-MM-DD-<uuid>
   165→	if len(sessionID1) < 11 {
   166→		t.Fatalf("Session ID too short: %s", sessionID1)
   167→	}
   168→	expectedSuffix := "-" + agentUUID
   169→	if sessionID1[len(sessionID1)-len(expectedSuffix):] != expectedSuffix {
   170→		t.Errorf("Session ID should end with %s, got %s", expectedSuffix, sessionID1)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	// Create a state file to simulate existing session
   174→	state := &SessionState{
   175→		SessionID:       sessionID1,
   176→		BaseCommit:      "test123",
   177→		StartedAt:       time.Now(),
   178→		CheckpointCount: 1,
   179→	}
   180→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   181→		t.Fatalf("SaveSessionState() error = %v", err)
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// Second call - should reuse existing session ID
   185→	sessionID2 := GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentUUID)
   186→
   187→	if sessionID2 != sessionID1 {
   188→		t.Errorf("Expected to reuse session ID %s, got %s", sessionID1, sessionID2)
   189→	}
   190→}
   191→
   192→// TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates tests cleanup of duplicate state files.
   193→func TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates(t *testing.T) {
   194→	dir := t.TempDir()
   195→	_, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   196→	if err != nil {
   197→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	t.Chdir(dir)
   201→
   202→	agentUUID := "b7d4dbd3-3e56-54bb-a70a-52ae77d94c6f"
   203→
   204→	// Simulate the bug: create state files from different days
   205→	oldSessionID := "2026-01-22-" + agentUUID
   206→	newSessionID := "2026-01-23-" + agentUUID
   207→
   208→	oldState := &SessionState{
   209→		SessionID:       oldSessionID,
   210→		BaseCommit:      "old123",
   211→		StartedAt:       time.Now().Add(-24 * time.Hour),
   212→		CheckpointCount: 2,
   213→	}
   214→	if err := SaveSessionState(oldState); err != nil {
   215→		t.Fatalf("SaveSessionState(old) error = %v", err)
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	newState := &SessionState{
   219→		SessionID:       newSessionID,
   220→		BaseCommit:      "new456",
   221→		StartedAt:       time.Now(),
   222→		CheckpointCount: 3,
   223→	}
   224→	if err := SaveSessionState(newState); err != nil {
   225→		t.Fatalf("SaveSessionState(new) error = %v", err)
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Call GetOrCreateEntireSessionID - should pick the newest and cleanup old
   229→	selectedID := GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(agentUUID)
   230→
   231→	// Should pick the most recent (2026-01-23)
   232→	if selectedID != newSessionID {
   233→		t.Errorf("Expected most recent session ID %s, got %s", newSessionID, selectedID)
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Old state file should be cleaned up
   237→	oldStateLoaded, err := LoadSessionState(oldSessionID)
   238→	if err != nil {
   239→		t.Fatalf("LoadSessionState(old) error = %v", err)
   240→	}
   241→	if oldStateLoaded != nil {
   242→		t.Errorf("Old state file should have been cleaned up, but still exists")
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	// New state file should still exist
   246→	newStateLoaded, err := LoadSessionState(newSessionID)
   247→	if err != nil {
   248→		t.Fatalf("LoadSessionState(new) error = %v", err)
   249→	}
   250→	if newStateLoaded == nil {
   251→		t.Errorf("New state file should exist")
   252→	}
   253→}
   254→

## Summary

Moved `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` from `strategy/` package to `session/` package ✅

### Changes Made

1. **`session/state.go`**
   - Added `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` function
   - Added `sort` import
   - Function uses existing `getGitCommonDir()` and `sessionStateDirName` constant

2. **`strategy/session_state.go`**
   - Removed `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` function  
   - Removed unused `sort` and `strings` imports

   - `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`: Added `session` import, updated 2 calls
   - `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`: Added `session` import, updated 2 calls
   - All now call `session.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` instead of `strategy.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()`

4. **Tests moved**
   - `session/session_test.go`: Added `TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID` and `TestGetOrCreateEntireSessionID_MultipleStates`
   - `strategy/session_state_test.go`: Removed those same tests
   - Updated tests to use `StateStore` API and `context.Background()`

### Why This is Better

The `session/` package is the **right domain owner** for session identity management:
- It already manages `.git/entire-sessions/` via `StateStore`
- It defines the `State` struct and session lifecycle
- `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` is about session identity, not strategy implementation

All tests pass ✅

> AGENT

Looking at the usages, I'd say **no, keep them in `paths`**. Here's why:

## Keep in `paths` Package

**1. Architectural Separation**
- `agent/claudecode` and `agent/geminicli` use these in their `TransformSessionID()` implementations
- They currently only import `agent` and `paths`
- Making them import `session` breaks layer separation (agents shouldn't know about session domain logic)

**2. Pure Utility Functions**
```go
func EntireSessionID(uuid string) string {
    return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02") + "-" + uuid
}
```
- No state, no side effects
- Simple string formatting
- More "utility" than "domain logic"

**3. Wide Usage (23 places)**
- Integration tests
- Agent implementations  
- Strategy code
- Hook handlers
- Moving would require changing 23 import statements

**4. `paths` is Ubiquitous**
- Most files already import `paths`
- It's the "common utilities" package
- Has no dependencies (lightweight)

## Division of Responsibility

**`paths` package:**
- String formatting utilities
- Path construction
- `EntireSessionID()`, `ModelSessionID()` - date formatting helpers

**`session` package:**
- Session lifecycle and state
- `.git/entire-sessions/` management
- `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` - smart lookup with fallback to `paths.EntireSessionID()`

This keeps the architecture clean: agents can format session IDs without knowing about session state management.

**Verdict:** Keep them in `paths` ✅

   218→func EntireSessionID(claudeSessionID string) string {
   219→	return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02") + "-" + claudeSessionID
   220→}
   221→
   222→// ModelSessionID extracts the Claude session ID from an Entire session ID.
   223→// The Entire session ID format is: YYYY-MM-DD-<claude-session-id>
   224→// Returns the original string if it doesn't match the expected format.
   225→func ModelSessionID(entireSessionID string) string {
   226→	// Expected format: YYYY-MM-DD-<model-session-id> (11 chars prefix: "2025-12-02-")
   227→	if len(entireSessionID) > 11 && entireSessionID[4] == '-' && entireSessionID[7] == '-' && entireSessionID[10] == '-' {
   228→		return entireSessionID[11:]
   229→	}
   230→	// Return as-is if not in expected format (backwards compatibility)
   231→	return entireSessionID
   232→}

   191→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.

   193→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {

   197→// GetClaudeProjectDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts

   199→//
   200→// In test environments, set ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR to override the default location.
   201→func GetClaudeProjectDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   202→	override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")
   203→	if override != "" {
   204→		return override, nil
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   208→	if err != nil {
   209→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   213→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   214→}

> AGENT

I've hit a **circular dependency problem**:

```
paths → session → checkpoint → paths ❌
```

If `paths` imports `session`, but `checkpoint` (which `session` imports) needs `paths.ValidateSessionID` for security validation, we get a cycle.

## Options

**1. Keep them in `paths`** (revert)
- Avoids circular dependency
- But you wanted them in `session` for domain reasons

**2. Create new `sessionid` package**
```
cmd/entire/cli/sessionid/
  - EntireSessionID()
  - ModelSessionID()
  - ValidateSessionID()
```
- Both `paths` and `session` import it
- Clean separation, no cycles
- Extra package just for 3 functions

**3. Move ValidateSessionID to checkpoint**
- checkpoint is what needs validation
- But then we have validation logic split across packages

Which approach do you prefer? I'd recommend option 2 (new `sessionid` package) - it's clean and makes the dependencies explicit.

> AGENT

The issue is that `checkpoint` validates session IDs, tool use IDs, and agent IDs for **path safety** (preventing path traversal attacks). 

## Best Solution: Move validators to `checkpoint` package

Since `checkpoint` is what needs the validation, and these are generic "path-safe ID" validators:

1. Move `ValidateSessionID`, `ValidateToolUseID`, `ValidateAgentID` to `checkpoint/validators.go`
2. Keep `EntireSessionID`, `ModelSessionID` in `session` (domain logic)
3. `session` can import `checkpoint` for validation without cycles

This makes sense because:
- `checkpoint` is a low-level storage primitive (no dependencies on domain logic)
- Validation is about storage safety, not session semantics
- Both `session` and `strategy` already import `checkpoint`

**Want me to implement this approach?** It's the cleanest way to break the cycle while keeping the session ID functions in `session`.

  "description": "Create validators.go in checkpoint package"

// Package session provides domain types and interfaces for managing AI coding sessions.
//
// A Session represents a unit of work with an AI agent (Claude Code, Cursor, etc.).
// Sessions can be nested - when a subagent runs, it creates a sub-session within
// the parent session.
//
// This package provides two levels of abstraction:
//
//  1. Sessions interface - High-level CRUD operations for full session objects,
//     combining session state with checkpoint data. This is the primary interface
//     for commands and the UI layer.
//
//  2. StateStore - Low-level primitive for managing session state files in
//     .git/entire-sessions/. This tracks active session state (base commit,
//     checkpoint count, etc.) but doesn't handle checkpoint content. Strategies
//     use this directly for performance-critical state management.
//
// See docs/architecture/sessions-and-checkpoints.md for the full domain model.
package session

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
	"entire.io/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
)

// Session represents a unit of work with an AI coding agent.
// Sessions can be nested when subagents are used.
```

    24→// currentSessionIDWithFallback returns the persisted Entire session ID when available.
    25→// Falls back to deriving from the model session ID for backward compatibility.
    26→func currentSessionIDWithFallback(modelSessionID string) string {
    27→	entireSessionID, err := paths.ReadCurrentSession()
    28→	if err != nil {
    29→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read current session: %v\n", err)
    30→	}
    31→	if entireSessionID != "" {
    32→		// Validate persisted session ID to fail-fast on corrupted files
    33→		if err := paths.ValidateSessionID(entireSessionID); err != nil {
    34→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: invalid persisted session ID: %v\n", err)
    35→			// Fall through to fallback
    36→		} else if modelSessionID == "" || paths.ModelSessionID(entireSessionID) == modelSessionID {
    37→			return entireSessionID
    38→		} else {
    39→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: persisted session ID does not match hook session ID\n")
    40→		}
    41→	}
    42→	if modelSessionID == "" {
    43→		return ""
    44→	}
    45→	// Get or create stable session ID (reuses existing if session resumed across days)
    46→	return session.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID(modelSessionID)
    47→}

     1→package checkpoint

     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"

     8→
     9→// pathSafeRegex matches alphanumeric characters, underscores, and hyphens only.
    10→// Used to validate IDs that will be used in file paths.
    11→var pathSafeRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$`)
    12→
    13→// ValidateSessionID validates that a session ID doesn't contain path separators.
    14→// This prevents path traversal attacks when session IDs are used in file paths.
    15→func ValidateSessionID(id string) error {
    16→	if id == "" {
    17→		return fmt.Errorf("session ID cannot be empty")
    18→	}
    19→	if strings.ContainsAny(id, "/\\") {
    20→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID %q: contains path separators", id)
    21→	}
    22→	return nil
    23→}
    24→
    25→// ValidateToolUseID validates that a tool use ID contains only safe characters for paths.
    26→// Tool use IDs can be UUIDs or prefixed identifiers like "toolu_xxx".
    27→func ValidateToolUseID(id string) error {
    28→	if id == "" {
    29→		return nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)
    30→	}
    31→	if !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {
    32→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid tool use ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only", id)
    33→	}
    34→	return nil
    35→}
    36→
    37→// ValidateAgentID validates that an agent ID contains only safe characters for paths.
    38→func ValidateAgentID(id string) error {
    39→	if id == "" {
    40→		return nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)
    41→	}
    42→	if !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {
    43→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid agent ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only", id)
    44→	}
    45→	return nil
    46→}
    47→

> AGENT

I see the problem. We have a circular dependency cycle:
- `checkpoint` → `logging` (for logging)
- `logging` → `checkpoint` (for validators)

The solution is to extract validators to a separate package with no dependencies. Let me create a new `validation` package:

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_016R96aQWM2EDHKfpEwZXUwD
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation/validators.go",
  "content": "// Package validation provides input validation functions for the Entire CLI.\n// This package has no dependencies to avoid import cycles.\npackage validation\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// pathSafeRegex matches alphanumeric characters, underscores, and hyphens only.\n// Used to validate IDs that will be used in file paths.\nvar pathSafeRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$`)\n\n// ValidateSessionID validates that a session ID doesn't contain path separators.\n// This prevents path traversal attacks when session IDs are used in file paths.\nfunc ValidateSessionID(id string) error {\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"session ID cannot be empty\")\n\t}\n\tif strings.ContainsAny(id, \"/\\\\\") {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid session ID %q: contains path separators\", id)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ValidateToolUseID validates that a tool use ID contains only safe characters for paths.\n// Tool use IDs can be UUIDs or prefixed identifiers like \"toolu_xxx\".\nfunc ValidateToolUseID(id string) error {\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)\n\t}\n\tif !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid tool use ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only\", id)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ValidateAgentID validates that an agent ID contains only safe characters for paths.\nfunc ValidateAgentID(id string) error {\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil // Empty is allowed (optional field)\n\t}\n\tif !pathSafeRegex.MatchString(id) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid agent ID %q: must be alphanumeric with underscores/hyphens only\", id)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n"
}
```

The issue is that `paths` importing `session` creates a cycle. Let me fix this by moving the session ID functions to a separate low-level package:

id: toolu_01HNzxG1wcYnuLLg67WUC7GD
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/sessionid/sessionid.go",
  "content": "// Package sessionid provides session ID formatting and transformation functions.\n// This package has minimal dependencies to avoid import cycles.\npackage sessionid\n\nimport (\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// EntireSessionID generates the full Entire session ID from an agent session UUID.\n// The format is: YYYY-MM-DD-<agent-session-uuid>\nfunc EntireSessionID(agentSessionUUID string) string {\n\treturn time.Now().Format(\"2006-01-02\") + \"-\" + agentSessionUUID\n}\n\n// ModelSessionID extracts the agent session UUID from an Entire session ID.\n// The Entire session ID format is: YYYY-MM-DD-<agent-session-uuid>\n// Returns the original string if it doesn't match the expected format.\nfunc ModelSessionID(entireSessionID string) string {\n\t// Expected format: YYYY-MM-DD-<agent-uuid> (11 chars prefix: \"2026-01-23-\")\n\tif len(entireSessionID) > 11 && entireSessionID[4] == '-' && entireSessionID[7] == '-' && entireSessionID[10] == '-' {\n\t\treturn entireSessionID[11:]\n\t}\n\t// Return as-is if not in expected format (backwards compatibility)\n\treturn entireSessionID\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Perfect! I've successfully completed the refactoring to fix the midnight-crossing bug and resolve all import cycles. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Changes Made

### 1. Fixed Midnight-Crossing Bug
**Problem**: Same Claude session across midnight creates duplicate Entire session IDs (e.g., `2026-01-22-uuid` vs `2026-01-23-uuid`), causing false concurrent session warnings.

**Solution**: Created `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()` in `session/state.go` that:
- Looks up existing state files by agent UUID
- Reuses the most recent existing session ID (preserving date prefix)
- Cleans up duplicate state files from the bug
- Never returns errors (always falls back to new ID)

### 2. Resolved Import Cycles
**Problem**: Moving session ID functions to `session` package created circular dependencies.

**Solution**: Created two new packages to break cycles:

#### `validation/` package
- Contains `ValidateSessionID`, `ValidateToolUseID`, `ValidateAgentID`
- No dependencies - prevents import cycles
- Used by `checkpoint`, `session`, `logging`, and `strategy` packages

#### `sessionid/` package  
- Contains `EntireSessionID` and `ModelSessionID` functions
- Minimal dependencies (only `time`)
- Used by `paths`, `session`, and all agent/hook handlers

### 3. Files Created
- `cmd/entire/cli/validation/validators.go` - Validation functions
- `cmd/entire/cli/sessionid/sessionid.go` - Session ID formatting

### 4. Files Modified
- `session/state.go` - Added `GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()`, updated imports
- `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` - Uses `session.GetOrCreateEntireSessionID()`, updated validators
- `logging/logger.go` - Updated to use `validation.ValidateSessionID`
- `paths/paths.go` - Updated to use `sessionid.EntireSessionID`
- `checkpoint/committed.go` - Updated to use `validation.*` validators
- `checkpoint/temporary.go` - Updated to use `validation.*` validators
- `strategy/session_state.go` - Updated to use `validation.ValidateSessionID`
- All agent files (`claude.go`, `gemini.go`) - Updated to use `sessionid.*`
- All integration tests - Updated to use `sessionid.*`
- `paths/paths_test.go` - Updated test to use `sessionid.*`
- `session/session_test.go` - Added tests for the new stable ID function

### 5. Test Results
All tests pass:
- `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/session` - ✅ PASS (including new tests)
- `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -short` - ✅ PASS (all packages)
- `go build ./cmd/entire` - ✅ Success

### Package Architecture
```
validation (no deps)
    ↑
    ├── checkpoint → logging → paths
    ├── session → sessionid (minimal deps)
    └── strategy
```

The refactoring successfully:
1. Fixes the midnight-crossing bug with stable session IDs
2. Eliminates all import cycles
3. Maintains clean package boundaries
4. Preserves all existing functionality
5. Passes all tests
